to make the maze imperfect maze you need to carve random walls from the rows and 
columns, but just avoid to carve borders walls.

specify a carve chance like 20% or 10% (exactly 0.2 or 0.1) 
then you loop over the width and height and generate a random integer between 
0 and 1 and if it is under < carve chance and the wall is not a border carve it to create 
an imperfect maze   ^HFOYEv6E
